Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the asymmetric structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 530. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 152 and converges at rate O(1/n^2).

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_530(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 530. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^152(S, F) is trivial. QED.
